2025-03-21T09:41:56.4966520Z Description:
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4966780Z   Remote Synchronization Tool
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4967100Z   
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4967540Z   This tool synchronizes two remote backends. The tool assumes that the intent is
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4967970Z   to have the destination match the source.
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4968270Z   
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4968630Z   If the destination has files that are not in the source, they will be deleted
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4969030Z   (or renamed if the retention option is set).
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4969300Z   
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4969670Z   If the destination has files that are also present in the source, but the files
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4970160Z   differ in size, or if the source files have a newer (more recent) timestamp,
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4970660Z   the destination files will be overwritten by the source files. Given that some
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4971150Z   backends do not allow for metadata or timestamp modification, and that the tool
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4971630Z   is run after backup, the destination files should always have a timestamp that
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4972120Z   is newer (or the same if run promptly) compared to the source files.
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4972450Z   
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4972770Z   If the force option is set, the destination will be overwritten by the source,
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4973290Z   regardless of the state of the files. It will also skip the initial comparison,
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4973710Z   and delete (or rename) all files in the destination.
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4974020Z   
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4974310Z   If the verify option is set, the files will be downloaded and compared after
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4974850Z   uploading to ensure that the files are correct. Files that already exist in the
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4975410Z   destination will be verified before being overwritten (if they seemingly match).
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4975810Z               
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4975940Z 
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4976120Z Usage:
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4976390Z   testhost <backend_src> <backend_dst> [options]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4976660Z 
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4976820Z Arguments:
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4977080Z   <backend_src>  The source backend string
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4977420Z   <backend_dst>  The destination backend string
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4977720Z 
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4977890Z Options:
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4978870Z   -y, --confirm, --yes               Automatically confirm the operation [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4979500Z   -d, --dry-run                      Do not actually write or delete files. If not set here, the global options will be checked [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4980480Z   --dst-options <dst-options>        Options for the destination backend. Each option is a key-value pair separated by an equals sign, e.g. --dst-options key1=value1 key2=value2 [default: empty] []
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4981200Z   -f, --force                        Force the synchronization [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4982090Z   --global-options <global-options>  Global options all backends. May be overridden by backend specific options (src-options, dst-options). Each option is a key-value pair separated by an equals sign, e.g. --global-options key1=value1 key2=value2 [default: empty] []
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4983760Z   --log-file <log-file>              The log file to write to. If not set here, global options will be checked [default: ""] []
2025-03-21T09:41:56.4984420Z   --log-level <log-level>            The log level to use. If not set here, global options will be checked [default: Information]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5000570Z   --parse-arguments-only             Only parse the arguments and then exit [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5001960Z   --progress                         Print progress to STDOUT [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5002540Z   --retention                        Toggles whether to keep old files. Any deletes will be renames instead [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5003090Z   --retry <retry>                    Number of times to retry on errors [default: 3]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5003790Z   --src-options <src-options>        Options for the source backend. Each option is a key-value pair separated by an equals sign, e.g. --src-options key1=value1 key2=value2 [default: empty] []
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5004800Z   --verify-contents                  Verify the contents of the files to decide whether the pre-existing destination files should be overwritten [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5005550Z   --verify-get-after-put             Verify the files after uploading them to ensure that they were uploaded correctly [default: False]
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5006180Z   --version                          Show version information
2025-03-21T09:41:56.5006570Z   -?, -h, --help                     Show help and usage information
